clc;
clear;
close all;

%% Loading Test Dataset


dataPath = 'UCI HAR Dataset/test/Inertial Signals/';

%% Body Acceleration

body_x = load([dataPath 'body_acc_x_test.txt']);
body_y = load([dataPath 'body_acc_y_test.txt']);
body_z = load([dataPath 'body_acc_z_test.txt']);

%% Total Acceleration

total_x = load([dataPath 'total_acc_x_test.txt']);
total_y = load([dataPath 'total_acc_y_test.txt']);
total_z = load([dataPath 'total_acc_z_test.txt']);

%% Activity Labels

labels = load('UCI HAR Dataset/test/y_test.txt');

%% Sampling Frequency

Fs = 50;

num_windows = size(body_x,1);

%% Butterworth Low-Pass Filter

Fc = 20;

filterOrder = 4;

Wn = Fc/(Fs/2);

[b,a] = butter(filterOrder,Wn,'low');

%% Feature Matrix

feature_matrix_test = zeros(num_windows,152);

%% Processing Test Windows

for i = 1:num_windows

    %% Body Acceleration

    bx = body_x(i,:);
    by = body_y(i,:);
    bz = body_z(i,:);

    %% Total Acceleration

    tx = total_x(i,:);
    ty = total_y(i,:);
    tz = total_z(i,:);

    %% DC Removal for Body Acceleration

    bx = bx - mean(bx);
    by = by - mean(by);
    bz = bz - mean(bz);

    %% Filter Body Acceleration

    bx = filtfilt(b,a,bx);
    by = filtfilt(b,a,by);
    bz = filtfilt(b,a,bz);

    %% Filter Total Acceleration

    % Do not remove DC from total acceleration

    tx = filtfilt(b,a,tx);
    ty = filtfilt(b,a,ty);
    tz = filtfilt(b,a,tz);

    %% Body Acceleration Magnitude

    body_mag = sqrt(bx.^2 + by.^2 + bz.^2);

    %% Total Acceleration Magnitude

    total_mag = sqrt(tx.^2 + ty.^2 + tz.^2);

    %% Feature Extraction

    f_bx = extractFeaturesandFFT(bx,Fs);

    f_by = extractFeaturesandFFT(by,Fs);

    f_bz = extractFeaturesandFFT(bz,Fs);

    f_tx = extractFeaturesandFFT(tx,Fs);

    f_ty = extractFeaturesandFFT(ty,Fs);

    f_tz = extractFeaturesandFFT(tz,Fs);

    f_bmag = extractFeaturesandFFT(body_mag,Fs);

    f_tmag = extractFeaturesandFFT(total_mag,Fs);

    %% Combine Features

    feature_matrix_test(i,:) = [

        f_bx ...
        f_by ...
        f_bz ...
        f_tx ...
        f_ty ...
        f_tz ...
        f_bmag ...
        f_tmag

    ];

end

%% Load Training Normalization Parameters

load('training_features.mat', ...
    'feature_mean', ...
    'feature_std');

%% Normalize Test Features

normalized_test_features = ...
    (feature_matrix_test - feature_mean) ./ feature_std;

%% Display Results

disp('Test feature extraction completed.'); %[output:7ba54f64]

disp('Test feature matrix size:'); %[output:5fd50777]

disp(size(feature_matrix_test)); %[output:689736dd]

disp('feature_matrix_test');

disp(feature_matrix_test); %[output:7d3fa668]

disp('Normalized test feature matrix size:'); %[output:8b77b127]

disp(size(normalized_test_features)); %[output:314db981]

disp('normalized_test_features');


disp(normalized_test_features); %[output:9e1a7433]

%% Save Test Features

save('test_features.mat', ...
    'normalized_test_features', ...
    'labels');

disp('Test features saved successfully.'); %[output:6b248320]




function features = extractFeaturesandFFT(signal,Fs)

N = length(signal);

%% Time Domain Features

mean_value = mean(signal);

std_value = std(signal);

rms_value = rms(signal);

max_value = max(signal);

min_value = min(signal);

range_value = max_value - min_value;

median_value = median(signal);

iqr_value = iqr(signal);

skewness_value = skewness(signal);

kurtosis_value = kurtosis(signal);

mean_abs_value = mean(abs(signal));

%% Zero Crossing Rate

zero_crossings = sum(abs(diff(signal > 0)));

zero_crossing_rate = zero_crossings / N;

%% FFT

X = fft(signal);

P2 = abs(X/N);

P1 = P2(1:N/2+1);

P1(2:end-1) = 2*P1(2:end-1);

%% Frequency Vector

f = Fs*(0:N/2)/N;

%% Dominant Frequency

[~,index] = max(P1(2:end));

index = index + 1;

dominant_frequency = f(index);

%% Spectral Energy

spectral_energy = sum(P1.^2);

%% Frequency Band Energy

band1 = f <= 3;

band2 = f > 3 & f <= 6;

band3 = f > 6 & f <= 10;

band4 = f > 10 & f <= 20;

energy_0_3 = sum(P1(band1).^2);

energy_3_6 = sum(P1(band2).^2);

energy_6_10 = sum(P1(band3).^2);

energy_10_20 = sum(P1(band4).^2);

%% Spectral Entropy

P = P1.^2;

P = P / sum(P);

P(P == 0) = eps;

spectral_entropy = -sum(P .* log2(P));

%% Feature Vector

features = [

    mean_value ...
    std_value ...
    rms_value ...
    max_value ...
    min_value ...
    range_value ...
    median_value ...
    iqr_value ...
    skewness_value ...
    kurtosis_value ...
    mean_abs_value ...
    zero_crossing_rate ...
    dominant_frequency ...
    spectral_energy ...
    energy_0_3 ...
    energy_3_6 ...
    energy_6_10 ...
    energy_10_20 ...
    spectral_entropy

];

end
